```
     return None, None
 
 
+def resolve_provider_credentials(
+    provider: str,
+    model: str,
+) -> Optional[dict]:
+    """Resolve a provider+model into a complete credentials bundle.
+
+    Thin wrapper around :func:`resolve_provider_client` that also derives
+    ``api_mode`` from the provider name.  Used by every code path that
+    needs to construct or switch to a new provider: fallback activation,
+    the ``switch_model`` tool, and per-task delegation overrides.
+
+    Returns a dict on success::
+
+        {
+            "provider": "openrouter",
+            "model": "anthropic/claude-sonnet-4-6",
+            "base_url": "https://...",
+            "api_key": "sk-...",
+            "api_mode": "chat_completions",
+            "client": <OpenAI>,       # ready-to-use client object
+        }
+
+    Returns ``None`` if the provider is not configured (no API key).
+    """
+    client, _ = resolve_provider_client(provider, model=model, raw_codex=True)
+    if client is None:
+        return None
+
+    api_mode = "chat_completions"
```

```
"""
Session Insights Engine for Hermes Agent.

Analyzes historical session data from the SQLite state database to produce
comprehensive usage insights — token consumption, cost estimates, tool usage
patterns, activity trends, model/platform breakdowns, and session metrics.

Inspired by Claude Code's /insights command, adapted for Hermes Agent's
multi-platform architecture with additional cost estimation and platform
breakdown capabilities.

Usage:
    from agent.insights import InsightsEngine
    engine = InsightsEngine(db)
    report = engine.generate(days=30)
    print(engine.format_terminal(report))
"""

import json
import time
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

# =========================================================================
# Model pricing (USD per million tokens) — approximate as of early 2026
# =========================================================================
MODEL_PRICING = {
    # OpenAI
    "gpt-4o": {"input": 2.50, "output": 10.00},
```

```
Args:
        task: Auxiliary task name ("compression", "vision", "web_extract",
              "session_search", "skills_hub", "mcp", "flush_memories").
              Reads provider:model from config/env. Ignored if provider is set.
        provider: Explicit provider override.
        model: Explicit model override.
        messages: Chat messages list.
        temperature: Sampling temperature (None = provider default).
        max_tokens: Max output tokens (handles max_tokens vs max_completion_tokens).
        tools: Tool definitions (for function calling).
        timeout: Request timeout in seconds.
        extra_body: Additional request body fields.

    Returns:
        Response object with .choices[0].message.content

    Raises:
        RuntimeError: If no provider is configured.
    """
    resolved_provider, resolved_model = _resolve_task_provider_model(
        task, provider, model)

    client, final_model = _get_cached_client(resolved_provider, resolved_model)
    if client is None:
        # Fallback: try openrouter
        if resolved_provider != "openrouter":
            logger.warning("Provider %s unavailable, falling back to openrouter",
                           resolved_provider)
            client, final_model = _get_cached_client(
                "openrouter", resolved_model or _OPENROUTER_MODEL)
    if client is None:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"No LLM provider configured for task={task} provider={resolved_provider}. "
            f"Run: hermes setup")

    kwargs = _build_call_kwargs(
        resolved_provider, final_model, messages,
        temperature=temperature, max_tokens=max_tokens,
        tools=tools, timeout=timeout, extra_body=extra_body)

    # Handle max_tokens vs max_completion_tokens retry
    try:
        return client.chat.completions.create(**kwargs)
    except Exception as first_err:
        err_str = str(first_err)
        if "max_tokens" in err_str or "unsupported_parameter" in err_str:
            kwargs.pop("max_tokens", None)
            kwargs["max_completion_tokens"] = max_tokens
            return client.chat.completions.create(**kwargs)
        raise


async def async_call_llm(
    task: str = None,
    *,
    provider: str = None,
    model: str = None,
    messages: list,
    temperature: float = None,
    max_tokens: int = None,
    tools: list = None,
    timeout: float = 30.0,
    extra_body: dict = None,
) -> Any:
    """Centralized asynchronous LLM call.

    Same as call_llm() but async. See call_llm() for full documentation.
    """
    resolved_provider, resolved_model = _resolve_task_provider_model(
        task, provider, model)

    client, final_model = _get_cached_client(
        resolved_provider, resolved_model, async_mode=True)
    if client is None:
        if resolved_provider != "openrouter":
            logger.warning("Provider %s unavailable, falling back to openrouter",
                           resolved_provider)
            client, final_model = _get_cached_client(
                "openrouter", resolved_model or _OPENROUTER_MODEL,
                async_mode=True)
    if client is None:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"No LLM provider configured for task={task} provider={resolved_provider}. "
            f"Run: hermes setup")

    kwargs = _build_call_kwargs(
        resolved_provider, final_model, messages,
        temperature=temperature, max_tokens=max_tokens,
        tools=tools, timeout=timeout, extra_body=extra_body)

    try:
        return await client.chat.completions.create(**kwargs)
    except Exception as first_err:
        err_str = str(first_err)
        if "max_tokens" in err_str or "unsupported_parameter" in err_str:
            kwargs.pop("max_tokens", None)
            kwargs["max_completion_tokens"] = max_tokens
            return await client.chat.completions.create(**kwargs)
        raise
```

```
     return None, None
 
 
+def resolve_provider_credentials(
+    provider: str,
+    model: str,
+) -> Optional[dict]:
+    """Resolve a provider+model into a complete credentials bundle.
+
+    Thin wrapper around :func:`resolve_provider_client` that also derives
+    ``api_mode`` from the provider name.  Used by every code path that
+    needs to construct or switch to a new provider: fallback activation,
+    the ``switch_model`` tool, and per-task delegation overrides.
+
+    Returns a dict on success::
+
+        {
+            "provider": "openrouter",
+            "model": "anthropic/claude-sonnet-4-6",
+            "base_url": "https://...",
+            "api_key": "sk-...",
+            "api_mode": "chat_completions",
+            "client": <OpenAI>,       # ready-to-use client object
+        }
+
+    Returns ``None`` if the provider is not configured (no API key).
+    """
+    client, _ = resolve_provider_client(provider, model=model, raw_codex=True)
+    if client is None:
+        return None
+
+    api_mode = "chat_completions"
+    if provider == "openai-codex":
+        api_mode = "codex_responses"
+    elif provider == "anthropic":
+        api_mode = "anthropic_messages"
+
+    return {
+        "provider": provider,
+        "model": model,
+        "base_url": str(client.base_url),
+        "api_key": client.api_key,
+        "api_mode": api_mode,
+        "client": client,
+    }
+
+
 # ── Public API ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

+# call call_llm() concurrently for compression, session search, etc.
+import threading
 _client_cache: Dict[tuple, tuple] = {}
+_client_cache_lock = threading.Lock()

     provider: str, model: str = None, async_mode: bool = False,
 ) -> Tuple[Optional[Any], Optional[str]]:
-    """Get or create a cached client for the given provider."""

+    Thread-safe: multiple subagent threads may resolve clients concurrently.

-    if cache_key in _client_cache:

+                client, default_model = _client_cache[cache_key]
```

```
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..9b79b5a
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tools/smart_model_tool.py
@@ -0,0 +1,119 @@
+"""
+smart_model — PoC tool that automatically picks and switches to the best model
+for a given task type.
+
+The agent calls this with a task description, and the tool picks an appropriate
+model (e.g., fast/cheap for simple tasks, powerful for complex ones) and switches.
+"""
+
+import json
+import logging
+
+from tools.registry import registry
+
+logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
+
+# Simple task-to-model mapping — customize as needed
+MODEL_PRESETS = {
+    "fast": {
+        "provider": "openrouter",
+        "model": "google/gemini-2.5-flash",
+        "description": "Fast and cheap — good for simple lookups, summaries, formatting",
+    },
+    "balanced": {
+        "provider": "openrouter",
+        "model": "anthropic/claude-sonnet-4",
+        "description": "Good balance of speed and capability",
+    },
+    "powerful": {
+        "provider": "openrouter",
+        "model": "anthropic/claude-opus-4",
+        "description": "Most capable — complex reasoning, coding, analysis",
+    },
+    "code": {
+        "provider": "openrouter",
+        "model": "anthropic/claude-sonnet-4",
+        "description": "Optimized for code generation and debugging",
+    },
+}
+
+
+def smart_model_handle(agent, preset: str, custom_provider: str = "", custom_model: str = "") -> str:
+    """Pick a model based on preset or custom provider/model, then switch."""
+
+    old_model = agent.model
+    old_provider = agent.provider
+
+    # Use custom if provided, otherwise look up preset
+    if custom_provider and custom_model:
+        provider = custom_provider
+        model = custom_model
+    elif preset in MODEL_PRESETS:
+        provider = MODEL_PRESETS[preset]["provider"]
+        model = MODEL_PRESETS[preset]["model"]
+    else:
+        return json.dumps({
+            "success": False,
+            "error": f"Unknown preset '{preset}'. Available: {list(MODEL_PRESETS.keys())}",
+            "current": {"provider": old_provider, "model": old_model},
+        })
+
+    # Already on this model? No-op.
+    if model == old_model and provider == old_provider:
+        return json.dumps({
+            "success": True,
+            "message": f"Already using {model}",
+            "current": {"provider": provider, "model": model},
+        })
+
+    # Delegate to agent's built-in switch
+    result = agent._switch_model(provider, model)
+    if result.get("success"):
+        result["preset"] = preset if preset in MODEL_PRESETS else "custom"
+    return json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False)
+
+
+# ── Schema ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+
+SMART_MODEL_SCHEMA = {
+    "name": "smart_model",
+    "description": (
+        "Switch to a model preset suited for the current task. "
+        "Presets: 'fast' (cheap/quick), 'balanced' (general), 'powerful' (complex reasoning), "
+        "'code' (programming). Or provide custom provider+model."
+    ),
+    "parameters": {
+        "type": "object",
+        "properties": {
+            "preset": {
+                "type": "string",
+                "enum": ["fast", "balanced", "powerful", "code", "custom"],
+                "description": "Model preset to use, or 'custom' with provider+model.",
+            },
+            "provider": {
+                "type": "string",
+                "description": "Custom provider (only with preset='custom'). E.g. 'openrouter'.",
+            },
+            "model": {
+                "type": "string",
+                "description": "Custom model ID (only with preset='custom'). E.g. 'openai/gpt-4o'.",
+            },
+        },
+        "required": ["preset"],
+    },
+}
+
+
+def _check_smart_model() -> bool:
+    return True
+
+
+registry.register(
+    name="smart_model",
+    toolset="smart_model",
+    schema=SMART_MODEL_SCHEMA,
+    handler=lambda args, **kw: json.dumps({
+        "error": "smart_model must be handled by the agent loop"
+    }),
+    check_fn=_check_smart_model,
+)
diff --git a/tools/switch_model_tool.py b/tools/switch_model_tool.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..9838005
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tools/switch_model_tool.py
@@ -0,0 +1,121 @@
+"""
+switch_model tool — lets the agent discover available models/providers
+and switch to a different one mid-conversation.
+
+Two actions:
+  - list: show current model, original model, and available providers+models
+  - switch: change to a different provider/model at runtime
+"""
+
+import json
+import logging
+
+from tools.registry import registry
+
+logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
+
+
+def switch_model_list(
+    current_provider: str,
+    current_model: str,
+    original: dict,
+) -> str:
+    """Return JSON describing current state and available providers/models."""
+    from hermes_cli.models import list_available_providers, curated_models_for_provider
+
+    available = []
+    for prov in list_available_providers():
+        models = curated_models_for_provider(prov["id"])
+        available.append({
+            "provider": prov["id"],
+            "label": prov["label"],
+            "authenticated": prov["authenticated"],
+            "models": [m_id for m_id, _ in models],
+        })
+
+    return json.dumps({
+        "current": {"provider": current_provider, "model": current_model},
+        "original": original,
+        "available_providers": available,
+    }, ensure_ascii=False)
+
+
+def switch_model_switch(
+    provider: str,
+    model: str,
+    reason: str,
+    agent,  # AIAgent — imported at call site to avoid circular import
+) -> str:
+    """Ask the agent to switch its model/provider in-place.
+
+    Delegates to agent._switch_model() which handles client construction.
+    Returns JSON the agent sees as its tool result.
+    """
+    if not provider or not model:
+        return json.dumps({
+            "success": False,
+            "error": "Both 'provider' and 'model' are required for switch action.",
+            "current": {"provider": agent.provider, "model": agent.model},
+        }, ensure_ascii=False)
+
+    if reason:
+        logger.info("switch_model reason: %s", reason)
+
+    result = agent._switch_model(provider, model)
+    return json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False)
+
+
+# ── Schema ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+
+SWITCH_MODEL_SCHEMA = {
+    "name": "switch_model",
+    "description": (
+        "Discover available LLM providers/models and switch to a different one "
+        "mid-conversation. Use action='list' to see what's available, then "
+        "action='switch' to change. Consider switching to a faster/cheaper model "
+        "for simple tasks, or a more capable model for complex reasoning."
+    ),
+    "parameters": {
+        "type": "object",
+        "properties": {
+            "action": {
+                "type": "string",
+                "enum": ["list", "switch"],
+                "description": "Action to perform: 'list' shows available models, 'switch' changes the active model.",
+            },
+            "provider": {
+                "type": "string",
+                "description": "Provider to switch to (required for 'switch'). E.g. 'openrouter', 'nous', 'anthropic'.",
+            },
+            "model": {
+                "type": "string",
+                "description": "Model ID to switch to (required for 'switch'). E.g. 'anthropic/claude-sonnet-4-6'.",
+            },
+            "reason": {
+                "type": "string",
+                "description": "Optional: why you're switching (logged for observability).",
+            },
+        },
+        "required": ["action"],
+    },
+}
+
+
+def _check_switch_model() -> bool:
+    """Always available when loaded."""
+    return True
+
+
+# ── Registration ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+
+registry.register(
+    name="switch_model",
+    toolset="switch_model",
+    schema=SWITCH_MODEL_SCHEMA,
+    # Handler is a stub — actual dispatch happens in run_agent._invoke_tool()
+    # because this tool needs agent-level state access.
+    handler=lambda args, **kw: json.dumps({
+        "error": "switch_model must be handled by the agent loop"
+    }),
+    check_fn=_check_switch_model,
+)
```

```
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..b479fcb
--- /dev/null
+++ b/gateway/control_api.py
@@ -0,0 +1,181 @@
+"""
+Local-only HTTP control API for the Hermes gateway.
+
+Runs alongside the gateway as a background aiohttp server on 127.0.0.1.
+Provides programmatic control over live agent sessions — model switching,
+session listing, etc.
+
+External tools (e.g., desloppify) can call these endpoints to influence
+a running Hermes agent without needing direct process access.
+
+Default port: 47823 (override with HERMES_CONTROL_PORT env var).
+"""
+
+import json
+import logging
+import os
+
+from aiohttp import web
+
+logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
+
+DEFAULT_PORT = 47823
+
+
+def _get_port() -> int:
+    return int(os.getenv("HERMES_CONTROL_PORT", str(DEFAULT_PORT)))
+
+
+def _json_response(data: dict, status: int = 200) -> web.Response:
+    return web.Response(
+        text=json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False),
+        content_type="application/json",
+        status=status,
+    )
+
+
+class ControlAPI:
+    """Lightweight control API that holds a reference to the GatewayRunner."""
+
+    def __init__(self, gateway_runner):
+        self.runner = gateway_runner
+        self.app = web.Application()
+        self._setup_routes()
+        self._site = None
+
+    def _setup_routes(self):
+        self.app.router.add_get("/sessions", self.list_sessions)
+        self.app.router.add_get("/sessions/{key}", self.get_session)
+        self.app.router.add_post("/sessions/{key}/switch-model", self.switch_model)
+        self.app.router.add_get("/health", self.health)
+
+    # ── Endpoints ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+
+    async def health(self, request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
+        return _json_response({"status": "ok"})
+
+    async def list_sessions(self, request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
+        """List all sessions, marking which have a live (running) agent."""
+        sessions = []
+        # Running agents (actively processing a message)
+        for key, agent in self.runner._running_agents.items():
+            sessions.append({
+                "session_key": key,
+                "status": "running",
+                "model": getattr(agent, "model", None),
+                "provider": getattr(agent, "provider", None),
+            })
+        return _json_response({"sessions": sessions})
+
+    async def get_session(self, request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
+        [REDACTED]["key"]
+        agent = self.runner._running_agents.get(key)
+        if not agent:
+            return _json_response(
+                {"error": f"No running agent for session '{key}'"},
+                status=404,
+            )
+        return _json_response({
+            "session_key": key,
+            "status": "running",
+            "model": getattr(agent, "model", None),
+            "provider": getattr(agent, "provider", None),
+        })
+
+    async def switch_model(self, request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
+        """Switch the model on a live agent session.
+
+        POST body: {"provider": "...", "model": "...", "reason": "..."}
+
+        If session_key is "_any", switches the first running agent found.
+        """
+        [REDACTED]["key"]
+
+        try:
+            body = await request.json()
+        except Exception:
+            return _json_response({"error": "Invalid JSON body"}, status=400)
+
+        provider = body.get("provider", "").strip()
+        model = body.get("model", "").strip()
+
+        if not provider or not model:
+            return _json_response(
+                {"error": "Both 'provider' and 'model' are required"},
+                status=400,
+            )
+
+        # _any: switch whichever agent is running
+        if key == "_any":
+            if not self.runner._running_agents:
+                return _json_response(
+                    {"error": "No running agents"},
+                    status=404,
+                )
+            key = next(iter(self.runner._running_agents))
+
+        agent = self.runner._running_agents.get(key)
+        if not agent:
+            return _json_response(
+                {"error": f"No running agent for session '{key}'"},
+                status=404,
+            )
+
+        reason = body.get("reason", "external control API")
+        logger.info(
+            "Control API: switch_model session=%s → %s/%s (reason: %s)",
+            key, provider, model, reason,
+        )
+
+        # Defer the switch so it happens between iterations, not mid-API-call.
+        # If the agent has the deferred mechanism, use it; otherwise switch directly.
+        if hasattr(agent, "_pending_model_switch"):
+            agent._pending_model_switch = {"provider": provider, "model": model}
+            return _json_response({
+                "success": True,
+                "message": "Model switch queued — will apply before next API call",
+                "current": {"provider": getattr(agent, "provider", ""), "model": getattr(agent, "model", "")},
+                "target": {"provider": provider, "model": model},
+            })
+        else:
+            result = agent._switch_model(provider, model)
+            status = 200 if result.get("success") else 500
+            return _json_response(result, status=status)
+
+    # ── Lifecycle ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+
+    async def start(self):
+        port = _get_port()
+        app_runner = web.AppRunner(self.app, access_log=None)
+        await app_runner.setup()
+        self._site = web.TCPSite(app_runner, "127.0.0.1", port)
+        try:
+            await self._site.start()
+            logger.info("Control API listening on http://127.0.0.1:%d", port)
+            # Write port file so external tools can discover us
+            self._write_port_file(port)
+        except OSError as e:
+            logger.warning("Control API failed to bind port %d: %s", port, e)
+
+    async def stop(self):
+        if self._site:
+            await self._site.stop()
+            self._remove_port_file()
+            logger.info("Control API stopped")
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _write_port_file(port: int):
+        path = os.path.expanduser("~/.hermes/control_api.port")
+        try:
+            with open(path, "w") as f:
+                f.write(str(port))
+        except OSError:
+            pass
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _remove_port_file():
+        path = os.path.expanduser("~/.hermes/control_api.port")
+        try:
+            os.remove(path)
+        except OSError:
+            pass
diff --git a/gateway/hermes_control_client.py b/gateway/hermes_control_client.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..21ce602
--- /dev/null
+++ b/gateway/hermes_control_client.py
@@ -0,0 +1,111 @@
+"""
+Hermes control client — lightweight module for external tools to control
+a running Hermes gateway.
+
+This is the module desloppify (or any external tool) imports to switch
+models, list sessions, etc. on a live Hermes instance.
+
+Usage:
+    from gateway.hermes_control_client import HermesControl
+
+    ctl = HermesControl()  # auto-discovers port from ~/.hermes/control_api.port
+
+    # Switch model on whichever agent is running
+    result = ctl.switch_model("openrouter", "anthropic/claude-sonnet-4")
+
+    # Switch model on a specific session
+    result = ctl.switch_model("openrouter", "anthropic/claude-sonnet-4",
+                              session_key="agent:main:telegram:12345")
+
+    # List running sessions
+    sessions = ctl.list_sessions()
+
+Zero dependencies beyond stdlib (uses urllib, not requests/aiohttp).
+"""
+
+import json
+import os
+import urllib.request
+import urllib.error
+from typing import Optional
+
+DEFAULT_PORT = 47823
+PORT_FILE = os.path.expanduser("~/.hermes/control_api.port")
+
+
+class HermesControl:
+    """Client for the Hermes gateway control API."""
+
+    def __init__(self, host: str = "127.0.0.1", port: Optional[int] = None):
+        if port is None:
+            port = self._discover_port()
+        self.base_url = f"http://{host}:{port}"
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _discover_port() -> int:
+        try:
+            with open(PORT_FILE) as f:
+                return int(f.read().strip())
+        except (OSError, ValueError):
+            return DEFAULT_PORT
+
+    def _request(self, method: str, path: str, body: dict = None) -> dict:
+        url = f"{self.base_url}{path}"
+        data = json.dumps(body).encode() if body else None
+        req = urllib.request.Request(
+            url,
+            data=data,
+            method=method,
+            headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"} if data else {},
+        )
+        try:
+            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=5) as resp:
+                return json.loads(resp.read())
+        except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
+            return json.loads(e.read())
+        except urllib.error.URLError as e:
+            return {"error": f"Cannot reach Hermes control API: {e.reason}"}
+
+    def health(self) -> dict:
+        """Check if the control API is running."""
+        return self._request("GET", "/health")
+
+    def list_sessions(self) -> dict:
+        """List all running agent sessions."""
+        return self._request("GET", "/sessions")
+
+    def get_session(self, session_key: str) -> dict:
+        """Get info about a specific session."""
+        return self._request("GET", f"/sessions/{session_key}")
+
+    def switch_model(
+        self,
+        provider: str,
+        model: str,
+        session_key: str = "_any",
+        reason: str = "",
+    ) -> dict:
+        """Switch the model on a running agent session.
+
+        Args:
+            provider: Provider ID (e.g., "openrouter", "anthropic")
+            model: Model ID (e.g., "anthropic/claude-sonnet-4")
+            session_key: Session to switch. "_any" switches the first running agent.
+            reason: Optional reason string (logged by Hermes).
+
+        Returns:
+            Dict with success/failure and previous/current model info.
+        """
+        return self._request("POST", f"/sessions/{session_key}/switch-model", {
+            "provider": provider,
+            "model": model,
+            "reason": reason or "desloppify",
+        })
+
+    def is_available(self) -> bool:
+        """Check if a Hermes gateway with control API is reachable."""
+        try:
+            result = self.health()
+            return result.get("status") == "ok"
+        except Exception:
+            return False
```

```
+    # ── Explicit model switch (switch_model tool) ───────────────────────────
+
+    def _switch_model(self, provider: str, model: str) -> dict:
+        """Switch the active model/provider in-place.
+
+        Called by the switch_model tool.  Returns a result dict
+        (success/failure + current state).
+        """
+        provider = provider.strip().lower()
+        model = model.strip()
+
+        old_provider = self.provider
+        old_model = self.model
+
+        try:
+            from agent.auxiliary_client import resolve_provider_credentials
+            creds = resolve_provider_credentials(provider, model)
+            if creds is None:
+                return {
+                    "success": False,
+                    "error": f"Provider '{provider}' is not configured. "
+                             f"Set the appropriate API key or run 'hermes setup'.",
+                    "current": {"provider": old_provider, "model": old_model},
+                }
+
+            self._apply_provider_credentials(creds)
+
+            print(
+                f"{self.log_prefix}🔄 Model switched: "
+                f"{old_model} → {model} via {provider}"
+            )
+            logging.info(
+                "Model switched: %s → %s (%s)",
+                old_model, model, provider,
+            )
+            return {
+                "success": True,
+                "previous": {"provider": old_provider, "model": old_model},
+                "current": {"provider": provider, "model": model},
+                "message": "Model switched successfully",
+            }
+        except Exception as e:
+            logging.error("Failed to switch model: %s", e)
+            return {
+                "success": False,
+                "error": f"Failed to switch model: {e}",
+                "current": {"provider": old_provider, "model": old_model},
+            }
+
```

```
commit 33f8d776a563adb9cec54d4a0d4807a6500b694a
Author: POM <[REDACTED]>
Date:   Sat Mar 14 06:42:12 2026 +0100

    fix: remove thread-unsafe redirect_stdout from delegate_tool
    
    contextlib.redirect_stdout mutates the global sys.stdout. When 3
    child agents start concurrently in batch delegation, the race between
    redirect_stdout and the spinner thread corrupts the file descriptor
    and causes a segfault.
    
    The child agent already runs with quiet_mode=True which suppresses
    its output — the redirect was redundant.
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.6 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>

diff --git a/tools/delegate_tool.py b/tools/delegate_tool.py
index 133770d..5ac60be 100644
--- a/tools/delegate_tool.py
+++ b/tools/delegate_tool.py
@@ -16,8 +16,6 @@ The parent's context only sees the delegation call and the summary result,
 never the child's intermediate tool calls or reasoning.
 """
 
-import contextlib
-import io
 import json
 import logging
 logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
@@ -258,10 +256,11 @@ def _run_single_child(
     child_progress_cb = getattr(child, "tool_progress_callback", None)
 
     try:
-        # Run with stdout/stderr suppressed to prevent interleaved output
-        devnull = io.StringIO()
-        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(devnull), contextlib.redirect_stderr(devnull):
-            result = child.run_conversation(user_message=goal)
+        # Child runs with quiet_mode=True so it suppresses its own output.
+        # Do NOT use contextlib.redirect_stdout here — it mutates the global
+        # sys.stdout and races with the spinner thread when multiple children
+        # start concurrently, causing segfaults.
+        result = child.run_conversation(user_message=goal)
 
         # Flush any remaining batched progress to gateway
         if child_progress_cb and hasattr(child_progress_cb, '_flush'):
@@ -491,11 +490,6 @@ def delegate_task(
         completed_count = 0
         spinner_ref = getattr(parent_agent, '_delegate_spinner', None)
 
-        # Save stdout/stderr before the executor — redirect_stdout in child
-        # threads races on sys.stdout and can leave it as devnull permanently.
-        _saved_stdout = sys.stdout
-        _saved_stderr = sys.stderr
-
         with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=MAX_CONCURRENT_CHILDREN) as executor:
             futures = {}
             for i, t, child in children:
@@ -547,10 +541,6 @@ def delegate_task(
                     except Exception as e:
                         logger.debug("Spinner update_text failed: %s", e)
 
-        # Restore stdout/stderr in case redirect_stdout race left them as devnull
-        sys.stdout = _saved_stdout
-        sys.stderr = _saved_stderr
-
         # Sort by task_index so results match input order
         results.sort(key=lambda r: r["task_index"])
```

```
+def _resolve_task_credentials(task: dict, default_creds: dict, parent_agent) -> dict:
+    """Resolve per-task credentials, falling back to config-level defaults.
+
+    If the task specifies a provider (and optionally model), resolve fresh
+    credentials via the shared :func:`resolve_provider_credentials` helper.
+    Otherwise use the default_creds from the delegation config.
+    """
+    task_provider = (task.get("provider") or "").strip().lower()
+    task_model = (task.get("model") or "").strip()
+
+    if not task_provider:
+        # No per-task provider — use config defaults, but allow model override
+        result = dict(default_creds)
+        if task_model:
+            result["model"] = task_model
+        return result
+
+    # Per-task provider specified — resolve credentials
+    try:
+        from agent.auxiliary_client import resolve_provider_credentials
+        creds = resolve_provider_credentials(task_provider, task_model or "")
+        if creds is None:
+            logger.warning(
+                "Per-task provider '%s' not configured, falling back to defaults",
+                task_provider,
+            )
+            return default_creds
+
+        return {
+            "model": creds["model"] or default_creds.get("model"),
+            "provider": creds["provider"],
+            "base_url": creds["base_url"],
+            "api_key": creds["api_key"],
+            "api_mode": creds["api_mode"],
+        }
+    except Exception as exc:
+        logger.error("Failed to resolve per-task provider '%s': %s", task_provider, exc)
+        return default_creds
+
```

```
1→"""
     2→switch_model tool — lets the agent discover available models/providers
     3→and switch to a different one mid-conversation.
     4→
     5→Two actions:
     6→  - list: show current model, original model, and available providers+models
     7→  - switch: change to a different provider/model at runtime
     8→"""
     9→
    10→import json
    11→import logging
    12→
    13→from tools.registry import registry
    14→
    15→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    16→
    17→
    18→def switch_model_list(
    19→    current_provider: str,
    20→    current_model: str,
    21→    original: dict,
    22→) -> str:
    23→    """Return JSON describing current state and available providers/models."""
    24→    from hermes_cli.models import list_available_providers, curated_models_for_provider
    25→
    26→    available = []
    27→    for prov in list_available_providers():
    28→        models = curated_models_for_provider(prov["id"])
    29→        available.append({
    30→            "provider": prov["id"],
    31→            "label": prov["label"],
    32→            "authenticated": prov["authenticated"],
    33→            "models": [m_id for m_id, _ in models],
    34→        })
    35→
    36→    return json.dumps({
    37→        "current": {"provider": current_provider, "model": current_model},
    38→        "original": original,
    39→        "available_providers": available,
    40→    }, ensure_ascii=False)
    41→
    42→
    43→def switch_model_switch(
    44→    provider: str,
    45→    model: str,
    46→    reason: str,
    47→    agent,  # AIAgent — imported at call site to avoid circular import
    48→) -> str:
    49→    """Ask the agent to switch its model/provider in-place.
    50→
    51→    Delegates to agent._switch_model() which handles client construction.
    52→    Returns JSON the agent sees as its tool result.
    53→    """
    54→    if not provider or not model:
    55→        return json.dumps({
    56→            "success": False,
    57→            "error": "Both 'provider' and 'model' are required for switch action.",
    58→            "current": {"provider": agent.provider, "model": agent.model},
    59→        }, ensure_ascii=False)
    60→
    61→    if reason:
    62→        logger.info("switch_model reason: %s", reason)
    63→
    64→    result = agent._switch_model(provider, model)
    65→    return json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False)
    66→
    67→
    68→# ── Schema ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    69→
    70→SWITCH_MODEL_SCHEMA = {
    71→    "name": "switch_model",
    72→    "description": (
    73→        "Discover available LLM providers/models and switch to a different one "
    74→        "mid-conversation. Use action='list' to see what's available, then "
    75→        "action='switch' to change. Consider switching to a faster/cheaper model "
    76→        "for simple tasks, or a more capable model for complex reasoning."
    77→    ),
    78→    "parameters": {
    79→        "type": "object",
    80→        "properties": {
    81→            "action": {
    82→                "type": "string",
    83→                "enum": ["list", "switch"],
    84→                "description": "Action to perform: 'list' shows available models, 'switch' changes the active model.",
    85→            },
    86→            "provider": {
    87→                "type": "string",
    88→                "description": "Provider to switch to (required for 'switch'). E.g. 'openrouter', 'nous', 'anthropic'.",
    89→            },
    90→            "model": {
    91→                "type": "string",
    92→                "description": "Model ID to switch to (required for 'switch'). E.g. 'anthropic/claude-sonnet-4-6'.",
    93→            },
    94→            "reason": {
    95→                "type": "string",
    96→                "description": "Optional: why you're switching (logged for observability).",
    97→            },
    98→        },
    99→        "required": ["action"],
   100→    },
   101→}
   102→
   103→
   104→def _check_switch_model() -> bool:
   105→    """Always available when loaded."""
   106→    return True
   107→
   108→
   109→# ── Registration ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   110→
   111→registry.register(
   112→    name="switch_model",
   113→    toolset="switch_model",
   114→    schema=SWITCH_MODEL_SCHEMA,
   115→    # Handler is a stub — actual dispatch happens in run_agent._invoke_tool()
   116→    # because this tool needs agent-level state access.
   117→    handler=lambda args, **kw: json.dumps({
   118→        "error": "switch_model must be handled by the agent loop"
   119→    }),
   120→    check_fn=_check_switch_model,
   121→)
   122→
```

```
1→"""
     2→smart_model — PoC tool that automatically picks and switches to the best model
     3→for a given task type.
     4→
     5→The agent calls this with a task description, and the tool picks an appropriate
     6→model (e.g., fast/cheap for simple tasks, powerful for complex ones) and switches.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→import json
    10→import logging
    11→
    12→from tools.registry import registry
    13→
    14→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    15→
    16→# Simple task-to-model mapping — customize as needed
    17→MODEL_PRESETS = {
    18→    "fast": {
    19→        "provider": "openrouter",
    20→        "model": "google/gemini-2.5-flash",
    21→        "description": "Fast and cheap — good for simple lookups, summaries, formatting",
    22→    },
    23→    "balanced": {
    24→        "provider": "openrouter",
    25→        "model": "anthropic/claude-sonnet-4",
    26→        "description": "Good balance of speed and capability",
    27→    },
    28→    "powerful": {
    29→        "provider": "openrouter",
    30→        "model": "anthropic/claude-opus-4",
    31→        "description": "Most capable — complex reasoning, coding, analysis",
    32→    },
    33→    "code": {
    34→        "provider": "openrouter",
    35→        "model": "anthropic/claude-sonnet-4",
    36→        "description": "Optimized for code generation and debugging",
    37→    },
    38→}
    39→
    40→
    41→def smart_model_handle(agent, preset: str, custom_provider: str = "", custom_model: str = "") -> str:
    42→    """Pick a model based on preset or custom provider/model, then switch."""
    43→
    44→    old_model = agent.model
    45→    old_provider = agent.provider
    46→
    47→    # Use custom if provided, otherwise look up preset
    48→    if custom_provider and custom_model:
    49→        provider = custom_provider
    50→        model = custom_model
    51→    elif preset in MODEL_PRESETS:
    52→        provider = MODEL_PRESETS[preset]["provider"]
    53→        model = MODEL_PRESETS[preset]["model"]
    54→    else:
    55→        return json.dumps({
    56→            "success": False,
    57→            "error": f"Unknown preset '{preset}'. Available: {list(MODEL_PRESETS.keys())}",
    58→            "current": {"provider": old_provider, "model": old_model},
    59→        })
    60→
    61→    # Already on this model? No-op.
    62→    if model == old_model and provider == old_provider:
    63→        return json.dumps({
    64→            "success": True,
    65→            "message": f"Already using {model}",
    66→            "current": {"provider": provider, "model": model},
    67→        })
    68→
    69→    # Delegate to agent's built-in switch
    70→    result = agent._switch_model(provider, model)
    71→    if result.get("success"):
    72→        result["preset"] = preset if preset in MODEL_PRESETS else "custom"
    73→    return json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False)
    74→
    75→
    76→# ── Schema ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    77→
    78→SMART_MODEL_SCHEMA = {
    79→    "name": "smart_model",
    80→    "description": (
    81→        "Switch to a model preset suited for the current task. "
    82→        "Presets: 'fast' (cheap/quick), 'balanced' (general), 'powerful' (complex reasoning), "
    83→        "'code' (programming). Or provide custom provider+model."
    84→    ),
    85→    "parameters": {
    86→        "type": "object",
    87→        "properties": {
    88→            "preset": {
    89→                "type": "string",
    90→                "enum": ["fast", "balanced", "powerful", "code", "custom"],
    91→                "description": "Model preset to use, or 'custom' with provider+model.",
    92→            },
    93→            "provider": {
    94→                "type": "string",
    95→                "description": "Custom provider (only with preset='custom'). E.g. 'openrouter'.",
    96→            },
    97→            "model": {
    98→                "type": "string",
    99→                "description": "Custom model ID (only with preset='custom'). E.g. 'openai/gpt-4o'.",
   100→            },
   101→        },
   102→        "required": ["preset"],
   103→    },
   104→}
   105→
   106→
   107→def _check_smart_model() -> bool:
   108→    return True
   109→
   110→
   111→registry.register(
   112→    name="smart_model",
   113→    toolset="smart_model",
   114→    schema=SMART_MODEL_SCHEMA,
   115→    handler=lambda args, **kw: json.dumps({
   116→        "error": "smart_model must be handled by the agent loop"
   117→    }),
   118→    check_fn=_check_smart_model,
   119→)
   120→
```

```
1→"""
     2→Local-only HTTP control API for the Hermes gateway.
     3→
     4→Runs alongside the gateway as a background aiohttp server on 127.0.0.1.
     5→Provides programmatic control over live agent sessions — model switching,
     6→session listing, etc.
     7→
     8→External tools (e.g., desloppify) can call these endpoints to influence
     9→a running Hermes agent without needing direct process access.
    10→
    11→Default port: 47823 (override with HERMES_CONTROL_PORT env var).
    12→"""
    13→
    14→import json
    15→import logging
    16→import os
    17→
    18→from aiohttp import web
    19→
    20→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    21→
    22→DEFAULT_PORT = 47823
    23→
    24→
    25→def _get_port() -> int:
    26→    return int(os.getenv("HERMES_CONTROL_PORT", str(DEFAULT_PORT)))
    27→
    28→
    29→def _json_response(data: dict, status: int = 200) -> web.Response:
    30→    return web.Response(
    31→        text=json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False),
    32→        content_type="application/json",
    33→        status=status,
    34→    )
    35→
    36→
    37→class ControlAPI:
    38→    """Lightweight control API that holds a reference to the GatewayRunner."""
    39→
    40→    def __init__(self, gateway_runner):
    41→        self.runner = gateway_runner
    42→        self.app = web.Application()
    43→        self._setup_routes()
    44→        self._site = None
    45→
    46→    def _setup_routes(self):
    47→        self.app.router.add_get("/sessions", self.list_sessions)
    48→        self.app.router.add_get("/sessions/{key}", self.get_session)
    49→        self.app.router.add_post("/sessions/{key}/switch-model", self.switch_model)
    50→        self.app.router.add_get("/health", self.health)
    51→
    52→    # ── Endpoints ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    53→
    54→    async def health(self, request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
    55→        return _json_response({"status": "ok"})
    56→
    57→    async def list_sessions(self, request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
    58→        """List all sessions, marking which have a live (running) agent."""
    59→        sessions = []
    60→        # Running agents (actively processing a message)
    61→        for key, agent in self.runner._running_agents.items():
    62→            sessions.append({
    63→                "session_key": key,
    64→                "status": "running",
    65→                "model": getattr(agent, "model", None),
    66→                "provider": getattr(agent, "provider", None),
    67→            })
    68→        return _json_response({"sessions": sessions})
    69→
    70→    async def get_session(self, request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
    71→        [REDACTED]["key"]
    72→        agent = self.runner._running_agents.get(key)
    73→        if not agent:
    74→            return _json_response(
    75→                {"error": f"No running agent for session '{key}'"},
    76→                status=404,
    77→            )
    78→        return _json_response({
    79→            "session_key": key,
    80→            "status": "running",
    81→            "model": getattr(agent, "model", None),
    82→            "provider": getattr(agent, "provider", None),
    83→        })
    84→
    85→    async def switch_model(self, request: web.Request) -> web.Response:
    86→        """Switch the model on a live agent session.
    87→
    88→        POST body: {"provider": "...", "model": "...", "reason": "..."}
    89→
    90→        If session_key is "_any", switches the first running agent found.
    91→        """
    92→        [REDACTED]["key"]
    93→
    94→        try:
    95→            body = await request.json()
    96→        except Exception:
    97→            return _json_response({"error": "Invalid JSON body"}, status=400)
    98→
    99→        provider = body.get("provider", "").strip()
   100→        model = body.get("model", "").strip()
   101→
   102→        if not provider or not model:
   103→            return _json_response(
   104→                {"error": "Both 'provider' and 'model' are required"},
   105→                status=400,
   106→            )
   107→
   108→        # _any: switch whichever agent is running
   109→        if key == "_any":
   110→            if not self.runner._running_agents:
   111→                return _json_response(
   112→                    {"error": "No running agents"},
   113→                    status=404,
   114→                )
   115→            key = next(iter(self.runner._running_agents))
   116→
   117→        agent = self.runner._running_agents.get(key)
   118→        if not agent:
   119→            return _json_response(
   120→                {"error": f"No running agent for session '{key}'"},
   121→                status=404,
   122→            )
   123→
   124→        reason = body.get("reason", "external control API")
   125→        logger.info(
   126→            "Control API: switch_model session=%s → %s/%s (reason: %s)",
   127→            key, provider, model, reason,
   128→        )
   129→
   130→        # Defer the switch so it happens between iterations, not mid-API-call.
   131→        # If the agent has the deferred mechanism, use it; otherwise switch directly.
   132→        if hasattr(agent, "_pending_model_switch"):
   133→            agent._pending_model_switch = {"provider": provider, "model": model}
   134→            return _json_response({
   135→                "success": True,
   136→                "message": "Model switch queued — will apply before next API call",
   137→                "current": {"provider": getattr(agent, "provider", ""), "model": getattr(agent, "model", "")},
   138→                "target": {"provider": provider, "model": model},
   139→            })
   140→        else:
   141→            result = agent._switch_model(provider, model)
   142→            status = 200 if result.get("success") else 500
   143→            return _json_response(result, status=status)
   144→
   145→    # ── Lifecycle ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   146→
   147→    async def start(self):
   148→        port = _get_port()
   149→        app_runner = web.AppRunner(self.app, access_log=None)
   150→        await app_runner.setup()
   151→        self._site = web.TCPSite(app_runner, "127.0.0.1", port)
   152→        try:
   153→            await self._site.start()
   154→            logger.info("Control API listening on http://127.0.0.1:%d", port)
   155→            # Write port file so external tools can discover us
   156→            self._write_port_file(port)
   157→        except OSError as e:
   158→            logger.warning("Control API failed to bind port %d: %s", port, e)
   159→
   160→    async def stop(self):
   161→        if self._site:
   162→            await self._site.stop()
   163→            self._remove_port_file()
   164→            logger.info("Control API stopped")
   165→
   166→    @staticmethod
   167→    def _write_port_file(port: int):
   168→        path = os.path.expanduser("~/.hermes/control_api.port")
   169→        try:
   170→            with open(path, "w") as f:
   171→                f.write(str(port))
   172→        except OSError:
   173→            pass
   174→
   175→    @staticmethod
   176→    def _remove_port_file():
   177→        path = os.path.expanduser("~/.hermes/control_api.port")
   178→        try:
   179→            os.remove(path)
   180→        except OSError:
   181→            pass
   182→
```

```
1→"""
     2→Hermes control client — lightweight module for external tools to control
     3→a running Hermes gateway.
     4→
     5→This is the module desloppify (or any external tool) imports to switch
     6→models, list sessions, etc. on a live Hermes instance.
     7→
     8→Usage:
     9→    from gateway.hermes_control_client import HermesControl
    10→
    11→    ctl = HermesControl()  # auto-discovers port from ~/.hermes/control_api.port
    12→
    13→    # Switch model on whichever agent is running
    14→    result = ctl.switch_model("openrouter", "anthropic/claude-sonnet-4")
    15→
    16→    # Switch model on a specific session
    17→    result = ctl.switch_model("openrouter", "anthropic/claude-sonnet-4",
    18→                              session_key="agent:main:telegram:12345")
    19→
    20→    # List running sessions
    21→    sessions = ctl.list_sessions()
    22→
    23→Zero dependencies beyond stdlib (uses urllib, not requests/aiohttp).
    24→"""
    25→
    26→import json
    27→import os
    28→import urllib.request
    29→import urllib.error
    30→from typing import Optional
    31→
    32→DEFAULT_PORT = 47823
    33→PORT_FILE = os.path.expanduser("~/.hermes/control_api.port")
    34→
    35→
    36→class HermesControl:
    37→    """Client for the Hermes gateway control API."""
    38→
    39→    def __init__(self, host: str = "127.0.0.1", port: Optional[int] = None):
    40→        if port is None:
    41→            port = self._discover_port()
    42→        self.base_url = f"http://{host}:{port}"
    43→
    44→    @staticmethod
    45→    def _discover_port() -> int:
    46→        try:
    47→            with open(PORT_FILE) as f:
    48→                return int(f.read().strip())
    49→        except (OSError, ValueError):
    50→            return DEFAULT_PORT
    51→
    52→    def _request(self, method: str, path: str, body: dict = None) -> dict:
    53→        url = f"{self.base_url}{path}"
    54→        data = json.dumps(body).encode() if body else None
    55→        req = urllib.request.Request(
    56→            url,
    57→            data=data,
    58→            method=method,
    59→            headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"} if data else {},
    60→        )
    61→        try:
    62→            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=5) as resp:
    63→                return json.loads(resp.read())
    64→        except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
    65→            return json.loads(e.read())
    66→        except urllib.error.URLError as e:
    67→            return {"error": f"Cannot reach Hermes control API: {e.reason}"}
    68→
    69→    def health(self) -> dict:
    70→        """Check if the control API is running."""
    71→        return self._request("GET", "/health")
    72→
    73→    def list_sessions(self) -> dict:
    74→        """List all running agent sessions."""
    75→        return self._request("GET", "/sessions")
    76→
    77→    def get_session(self, session_key: str) -> dict:
    78→        """Get info about a specific session."""
    79→        return self._request("GET", f"/sessions/{session_key}")
    80→
    81→    def switch_model(
    82→        self,
    83→        provider: str,
    84→        model: str,
    85→        session_key: str = "_any",
    86→        reason: str = "",
    87→    ) -> dict:
    88→        """Switch the model on a running agent session.
    89→
    90→        Args:
    91→            provider: Provider ID (e.g., "openrouter", "anthropic")
    92→            model: Model ID (e.g., "anthropic/claude-sonnet-4")
    93→            session_key: Session to switch. "_any" switches the first running agent.
    94→            reason: Optional reason string (logged by Hermes).
    95→
    96→        Returns:
    97→            Dict with success/failure and previous/current model info.
    98→        """
    99→        return self._request("POST", f"/sessions/{session_key}/switch-model", {
   100→            "provider": provider,
   101→            "model": model,
   102→            "reason": reason or "desloppify",
   103→        })
   104→
   105→    def is_available(self) -> bool:
   106→        """Check if a Hermes gateway with control API is reachable."""
   107→        try:
   108→            result = self.health()
   109→            return result.get("status") == "ok"
   110→        except Exception:
   111→            return False
   112→
```

```
1→#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2→"""
     3→Delegate Tool -- Subagent Architecture
     4→
     5→Spawns child AIAgent instances with isolated context, restricted toolsets,
     6→and their own terminal sessions. Supports single-task and batch (parallel)
     7→modes. The parent blocks until all children complete.
     8→
     9→Each child gets:
    10→  - A fresh conversation (no parent history)
    11→  - Its own task_id (own terminal session, file ops cache)
    12→  - A restricted toolset (configurable, with blocked tools always stripped)
    13→  - A focused system prompt built from the delegated goal + context
    14→
    15→The parent's context only sees the delegation call and the summary result,
    16→never the child's intermediate tool calls or reasoning.
    17→"""
    18→
    19→import json
    20→import logging
    21→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    22→import sys
    23→import threading
    24→import time
    25→from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed
    26→from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
    27→
    28→
    29→# Tools that children must never have access to
    30→DELEGATE_BLOCKED_TOOLS = frozenset([
    31→    "delegate_task",   # no recursive delegation
    32→    "clarify",         # no user interaction
    33→    "memory",          # no writes to shared MEMORY.md
    34→    "send_message",    # no cross-platform side effects
    35→    "execute_code",    # children should reason step-by-step, not write scripts
    36→])
    37→
    38→MAX_CONCURRENT_CHILDREN = 3
    39→MAX_DEPTH = 2  # parent (0) -> child (1) -> grandchild rejected (2)
    40→DEFAULT_MAX_ITERATIONS = 50
    41→DEFAULT_TOOLSETS = ["terminal", "file", "web"]
    42→
    43→
    44→def check_delegate_requirements() -> bool:
    45→    """Delegation has no external requirements -- always available."""
    46→    return True
    47→
    48→
    49→def _build_child_system_prompt(goal: str, context: Optional[str] = None) -> str:
    50→    """Build a focused system prompt for a child agent."""
    51→    parts = [
    52→        "You are a focused subagent working on a specific delegated task.",
    53→        "",
    54→        f"YOUR TASK:\n{goal}",
    55→    ]
    56→    if context and context.strip():
    57→        parts.append(f"\nCONTEXT:\n{context}")
    58→    parts.append(
    59→        "\nComplete this task using the tools available to you. "
    60→        "When finished, provide a clear, concise summary of:\n"
    61→        "- What you did\n"
    62→        "- What you found or accomplished\n"
    63→        "- Any files you created or modified\n"
    64→        "- Any issues encountered\n\n"
    65→        "Be thorough but concise -- your response is returned to the "
    66→        "parent agent as a summary."
    67→    )
    68→    return "\n".join(parts)
    69→
    70→
    71→def _strip_blocked_tools(toolsets: List[str]) -> List[str]:
    72→    """Remove toolsets that contain only blocked tools."""
    73→    blocked_toolset_names = {
    74→        "delegation", "clarify", "memory", "code_execution",
    75→    }
    76→    return [t for t in toolsets if t not in blocked_toolset_names]
    77→
    78→
    79→def _build_child_progress_callback(task_index: int, parent_agent, task_count: int = 1) -> Optional[callable]:
    80→    """Build a callback that relays child agent tool calls to the parent display.
    81→
    82→    Two display paths:
    83→      CLI:     prints tree-view lines above the parent's delegation spinner
    84→      Gateway: batches tool names and relays to parent's progress callback
    85→
    86→    Returns None if no display mechanism is available, in which case the
    87→    child agent runs with no progress callback (identical to current behavior).
    88→    """
    89→    spinner = getattr(parent_agent, '_delegate_spinner', None)
    90→    parent_cb = getattr(parent_agent, 'tool_progress_callback', None)
    91→
    92→    if not spinner and not parent_cb:
    93→        return None  # No display → no callback → zero behavior change
    94→
    95→    # Show 1-indexed prefix only in batch mode (multiple tasks)
    96→    prefix = f"[{task_index + 1}] " if task_count > 1 else ""
    97→
    98→    # Gateway: batch tool names, flush periodically
    99→    _BATCH_SIZE = 5
   100→    _batch: List[str] = []
   101→
   102→    def _callback(tool_name: str, preview: str = None):
   103→        # Special "_thinking" event: model produced text content (reasoning)
   104→        if tool_name == "_thinking":
   105→            if spinner:
   106→                short = (preview[:55] + "...") if preview and len(preview) > 55 else (preview or "")
   107→                try:
   108→                    spinner.print_above(f" {prefix}├─ 💭 \"{short}\"")
   109→                except Exception as e:
   110→                    logger.debug("Spinner print_above failed: %s", e)
   111→            # Don't relay thinking to gateway (too noisy for chat)
   112→            return
   113→
   114→        # Regular tool call event
   115→        if spinner:
   116→            short = (preview[:35] + "...") if preview and len(preview) > 35 else (preview or "")
   117→            tool_emojis = {
   118→                "terminal": "💻", "web_search": "🔍", "web_extract": "📄",
   119→                "read_file": "📖", "write_file": "✍️", "patch": "🔧",
   120→                "search_files": "🔎", "list_directory": "📂",
   121→                "browser_navigate": "🌐", "browser_click": "👆",
   122→                "text_to_speech": "🔊", "image_generate": "🎨",
   123→                "vision_analyze": "👁️", "process": "⚙️",
   124→            }
   125→            emoji = tool_emojis.get(tool_name, "⚡")
   126→            line = f" {prefix}├─ {emoji} {tool_name}"
   127→            if short:
   128→                line += f"  \"{short}\""
   129→            try:
   130→                spinner.print_above(line)
   131→            except Exception as e:
   132→                logger.debug("Spinner print_above failed: %s", e)
   133→
   134→        if parent_cb:
   135→            _batch.append(tool_name)
   136→            if len(_batch) >= _BATCH_SIZE:
   137→                summary = ", ".join(_batch)
   138→                try:
   139→                    parent_cb("subagent_progress", f"🔀 {prefix}{summary}")
   140→                except Exception as e:
   141→                    logger.debug("Parent callback failed: %s", e)
   142→                _batch.clear()
   143→
   144→    def _flush():
   145→        """Flush remaining batched tool names to gateway on completion."""
   146→        if parent_cb and _batch:
   147→            summary = ", ".join(_batch)
   148→            try:
   149→                parent_cb("subagent_progress", f"🔀 {prefix}{summary}")
   150→            except Exception as e:
   151→                logger.debug("Parent callback flush failed: %s", e)
   152→            _batch.clear()
   153→
   154→    _callback._flush = _flush
   155→    return _callback
   156→
   157→
   158→def _build_child_agent(
   159→    task_index: int,
   160→    goal: str,
   161→    context: Optional[str],
   162→    toolsets: Optional[List[str]],
   163→    model: Optional[str],
   164→    max_iterations: int,
   165→    parent_agent,
   166→    task_count: int = 1,
   167→    override_provider: Optional[str] = None,
   168→    override_base_url: Optional[str] = None,
   169→    override_api_key: Optional[str] = None,
   170→    override_api_mode: Optional[str] = None,
   171→) -> "AIAgent":
   172→    """Build a child AIAgent in the calling thread.
   173→
   174→    Constructing OpenAI/httpx clients is NOT thread-safe (SSL init,
   175→    connection pool setup, etc.), so this MUST run in the main thread.
   176→    The returned agent can then be handed to a worker thread for
   177→    ``run_conversation()``.
   178→    """
   179→    from run_agent import AIAgent
   180→
   181→    # When no explicit toolsets given, inherit from parent's enabled toolsets
   182→    if toolsets:
   183→        child_toolsets = _strip_blocked_tools(toolsets)
   184→    elif parent_agent and getattr(parent_agent, "enabled_toolsets", None):
   185→        child_toolsets = _strip_blocked_tools(parent_agent.enabled_toolsets)
   186→    else:
   187→        child_toolsets = _strip_blocked_tools(DEFAULT_TOOLSETS)
   188→
   189→    child_prompt = _build_child_system_prompt(goal, context)
   190→
   191→    parent_api_key = getattr(parent_agent, "api_key", None)
   192→    if (not parent_api_key) and hasattr(parent_agent, "_client_kwargs"):
   193→        [REDACTED]("api_key")
   194→
   195→    child_progress_cb = _build_child_progress_callback(task_index, parent_agent, task_count)
   196→    shared_budget = getattr(parent_agent, "iteration_budget", None)
   197→
   198→    effective_model = model or parent_agent.model
   199→    effective_provider = override_provider or getattr(parent_agent, "provider", None)
   200→    effective_base_url = override_base_url or parent_agent.base_url
   201→    [REDACTED] or parent_api_key
   202→    effective_api_mode = override_api_mode or getattr(parent_agent, "api_mode", None)
   203→
   204→    child = AIAgent(
   205→        base_url=effective_base_url,
   206→        [REDACTED],
   207→        model=effective_model,
   208→        provider=effective_provider,
   209→        api_mode=effective_api_mode,
   210→        max_iterations=max_iterations,
   211→        max_tokens=getattr(parent_agent, "max_tokens", None),
   212→        reasoning_config=getattr(parent_agent, "reasoning_config", None),
   213→        prefill_messages=getattr(parent_agent, "prefill_messages", None),
   214→        enabled_toolsets=child_toolsets,
   215→        quiet_mode=True,
   216→        ephemeral_system_prompt=child_prompt,
   217→        log_prefix=f"[subagent-{task_index}]",
   218→        platform=parent_agent.platform,
   219→        skip_context_files=True,
   220→        skip_memory=True,
   221→        clarify_callback=None,
   222→        session_db=getattr(parent_agent, '_session_db', None),
   223→        providers_allowed=parent_agent.providers_allowed,
   224→        providers_ignored=parent_agent.providers_ignored,
   225→        providers_order=parent_agent.providers_order,
   226→        provider_sort=parent_agent.provider_sort,
   227→        tool_progress_callback=child_progress_cb,
   228→        iteration_budget=shared_budget,
   229→    )
   230→
   231→    child._delegate_depth = getattr(parent_agent, '_delegate_depth', 0) + 1
   232→
   233→    if hasattr(parent_agent, '_active_children'):
   234→        lock = getattr(parent_agent, '_active_children_lock', None)
   235→        if lock:
   236→            with lock:
   237→                parent_agent._active_children.append(child)
   238→        else:
   239→            parent_agent._active_children.append(child)
   240→
   241→    return child
   242→
   243→
   244→def _run_single_child(
   245→    task_index: int,
   246→    goal: str,
   247→    child: "AIAgent",
   248→    parent_agent,
   249→) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   250→    """Run a pre-built child agent's conversation. Safe to call from a thread.
   251→
   252→    The child agent (and its httpx client) was already constructed in the
   253→    main thread by ``_build_child_agent()``.
   254→    """
   255→    child_start = time.monotonic()
   256→    child_progress_cb = getattr(child, "tool_progress_callback", None)
   257→
   258→    try:
   259→        # Child runs with quiet_mode=True so it suppresses its own output.
   260→        # Do NOT use contextlib.redirect_stdout here — it mutates the global
   261→        # sys.stdout and races with the spinner thread when multiple children
   262→        # start concurrently, causing segfaults.
   263→        result = child.run_conversation(user_message=goal)
   264→
   265→        # Flush any remaining batched progress to gateway
   266→        if child_progress_cb and hasattr(child_progress_cb, '_flush'):
   267→            try:
   268→                child_progress_cb._flush()
   269→            except Exception as e:
   270→                logger.debug("Progress callback flush failed: %s", e)
   271→
   272→        duration = round(time.monotonic() - child_start, 2)
   273→
   274→        summary = result.get("final_response") or ""
   275→        completed = result.get("completed", False)
   276→        interrupted = result.get("interrupted", False)
   277→        api_calls = result.get("api_calls", 0)
   278→
   279→        if interrupted:
   280→            status = "interrupted"
   281→        elif completed and summary:
   282→            status = "completed"
   283→        else:
   284→            status = "failed"
   285→
   286→        # Build tool trace from conversation messages (already in memory).
   287→        # Uses tool_call_id to correctly pair parallel tool calls with results.
   288→        tool_trace: list[Dict[str, Any]] = []
   289→        trace_by_id: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
   290→        messages = result.get("messages") or []
   291→        if isinstance(messages, list):
   292→            for msg in messages:
   293→                if not isinstance(msg, dict):
   294→                    continue
   295→                if msg.get("role") == "assistant":
   296→                    for tc in (msg.get("tool_calls") or []):
   297→                        fn = tc.get("function", {})
   298→                        entry_t = {
   299→                            "tool": fn.get("name", "unknown"),
   300→                            "args_bytes": len(fn.get("arguments", "")),
```

```
   121→        """Create tables and FTS if they don't exist, run migrations."""
   122→        cursor = self._conn.cursor()
   123→
   124→        cursor.executescript(SCHEMA_SQL)
   125→
   126→        # Check schema version and run migrations
   127→        cursor.execute("SELECT version FROM schema_version LIMIT 1")
   128→        row = cursor.fetchone()
   129→        if row is None:
   130→            cursor.execute("INSERT INTO schema_version (version) VALUES (?)", (SCHEMA_VERSION,))

   152→                        "ON sessions(title) WHERE title IS NOT NULL"
   153→                    )
   154→                except sqlite3.OperationalError:
   155→                    pass  # Index already exists
   156→                cursor.execute("UPDATE schema_version SET version = 4")
   157→
   158→        # Unique title index — always ensure it exists (safe to run after migrations
   159→        # since the title column is guaranteed to exist at this point)
   160→        try:
   161→            cursor.execute(
   162→                "CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_sessions_title_unique "
   163→                "ON sessions(title) WHERE title IS NOT NULL"

   165→        except sqlite3.OperationalError:

   173→
   174→        self._conn.commit()
```

+---
+name: desloppify
+description: >
+  Codebase health scanner and technical debt tracker. Use when the user asks
+  about code quality, technical debt, dead code, large files, god classes,
+  duplicate functions, code smells, naming issues, import cycles, or coupling
+  problems. Also use when asked for a health score, what to fix next, or to
+  create a cleanup plan. Supports 29 languages.
+allowed-tools: Bash(desloppify *)
+---
+
+<!-- desloppify-begin -->
+<!-- desloppify-skill-version: 5 -->
+
+# Desloppify
+
+## 1. Your Job
+
+Maximise the **strict score** honestly. Your main cycle: **scan → plan → execute → rescan**. Follow the scan output's **INSTRUCTIONS FOR AGENTS** — don't substitute your own analysis.
+
+**Don't be lazy.** Do large refactors and small detailed fixes with equal energy. If it takes touching 20 files, touch 20 files. If it's a one-line change, make it. No task is too big or too small — fix things properly, not minimally.
+
+## 2. The Workflow
+
+Three phases, repeated as a cycle.
+
+### Phase 1: Scan and review — understand the codebase
+
+```bash
+desloppify scan --path .       # analyse the codebase
+desloppify status              # check scores — are we at target?
+```
+
+The scan will tell you if subjective dimensions need review. Follow its instructions. To trigger a review manually:
+```bash
+desloppify review --prepare    # then follow your runner's review workflow
+```
+
+### Phase 2: Plan — decide what to work on
+
+After reviews, triage stages and plan creation appear in the execution queue surfaced by `next`. Complete them in order — `next` tells you what each stage expects in the `--report`:
+```bash
+desloppify next                                        # shows the next execution workflow step
+desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "themes and root causes..."
+desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "comparison against completed work..."
+desloppify plan triage --stage organize --report "summary of priorities..."
+desloppify plan triage --complete --strategy "execution plan..."
+```
+
+For automated triage: `desloppify plan triage --run-stages --runner codex` (Codex) or `--runner claude` (Claude). Options: `--only-stages`, `--dry-run`, `--stage-timeout-seconds`.
+
+Then shape the queue. **The plan shapes everything `next` gives you** — `next` is the execution queue, not the full backlog. Don't skip this step.
+
+```bash
+desloppify plan                          # see the living plan details
+desloppify plan queue                    # compact execution queue view
+desloppify plan reorder <pat> top        # reorder — what unblocks the most?
+desloppify plan cluster create <name>    # group related issues to batch-fix
+desloppify plan focus <cluster>          # scope next to one cluster
+desloppify plan skip <pat>              # defer — hide from next
+```
+
+### Phase 3: Execute — grind the queue to completion
+
+Trust the plan and execute. Don't rescan mid-queue — finish the queue first.
+
+**Branch first.** Create a dedicated branch — never commit health work directly to main:
+```bash
+git checkout -b desloppify/code-health    # or desloppify/<focus-area>
+desloppify config set commit_pr 42        # link a PR for auto-updated descriptions
+```
+
+**The loop:**
+```bash
+# 1. Get the next item from the execution queue
+desloppify next
+
+# 2. Fix the issue in code
+
+# 3. Resolve it (next shows the exact command including required attestation)
+
+# 4. When you have a logical batch, commit and record
+git add <files> && git commit -m "desloppify: fix 3 deferred_import findings"
+desloppify plan commit-log record      # moves findings uncommitted → committed, updates PR
+
+# 5. Push periodically
+git push -u origin desloppify/code-health
+
+# 6. Repeat until the queue is empty
+```
+
+Score may temporarily drop after fixes — cascade effects are normal, keep going.
+If `next` suggests an auto-fixer, run `desloppify autofix <fixer> --dry-run` to preview, then apply.
+
+**When the queue is clear, go back to Phase 1.** New issues will surface, cascades will have resolved, priorities will have shifted. This is the cycle.
+
+## 3. Reference
+
+### Key concepts
+
+- **Tiers**: T1 auto-fix → T2 quick manual → T3 judgment call → T4 major refactor.
+- **Auto-clusters**: related findings are auto-grouped in `next`. Drill in with `next --cluster <name>`.
+- **Zones**: production/script (scored), test/config/generated/vendor (not scored). Fix with `zone set`.
+- **Wontfix cost**: widens the lenient↔strict gap. Challenge past decisions when the gap grows.
+
+### Scoring
+
+Overall score = **25% mechanical** + **75% subjective**.
+
+- **Mechanical (25%)**: auto-detected issues — duplication, dead code, smells, unused imports, security. Fixed by changing code and rescanning.
+- **Subjective (75%)**: design quality review — naming, error handling, abstractions, clarity. Starts at **0%** until reviewed. The scan will prompt you when a review is needed.
+- **Strict score** is the north star: wontfix items count as open. The gap between overall and strict is your wontfix debt.
+- **Score types**: overall (lenient), strict (wontfix counts), objective (mechanical only), verified (confirmed fixes only).
+
+### Reviews
+
+Four paths to get subjective scores:
+
+- **Local runner (Codex)**: `desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` — automated end-to-end.
+- **Local runner (Claude)**: `desloppify review --prepare` → launch parallel subagents → `desloppify review --import merged.json` — see skill doc overlay for details.
+- **Cloud/external**: `desloppify review --external-start --external-runner claude` → follow session template → `--external-submit`.
+- **Manual path**: `desloppify review --prepare` → review per dimension → `desloppify review --import file.json`.
+
+- Import first, fix after — import creates tracked state entries for correlation.
+- Target-matching scores trigger auto-reset to prevent gaming. Use the blind-review workflow described in your agent overlay doc (e.g. `docs/CLAUDE.md`, `docs/HERMES.md`).
+- Even moderate scores (60-80) dramatically improve overall health.
+- Stale dimensions auto-surface in `next` — just follow the queue.
+
+**Integrity rules:** Score from evidence only — no prior chat context, score history, or target-threshold anchoring. When evidence is mixed, score lower and explain uncertainty. Assess every requested dimension; never drop one.
+
+#### Review output format
+
+Return machine-readable JSON for review imports. For `--external-submit`, include `session` from the generated template:
+
+```json
+{
+  "session": {
+    "id": "<session_id_from_template>",
+    "token": "<session_token_from_template>"
+  },
+  "assessments": {
+    "<dimension_from_query>": 0
+  },
+  "findings": [
+    {
+      "dimension": "<dimension_from_query>",
+      "identifier": "short_id",
+      "summary": "one-line defect summary",
+      "related_files": ["relative/path/to/file.py"],
+      "evidence": ["specific code observation"],
+      "suggestion": "concrete fix recommendation",
+      "confidence": "high|medium|low"
+    }
+  ]
+}
+```
+
+`findings` MUST match `query.system_prompt` exactly (including `related_files`, `evidence`, and `suggestion`). Use `"findings": []` when no defects found. Import is fail-closed: invalid findings abort unless `--allow-partial` is passed. Assessment scores are auto-applied from trusted internal or cloud session imports. Legacy `--attested-external` remains supported.
+
+#### Import paths

```
   477→        )
   478→        if result.returncode == 0:
   479→            return result.stdout.strip()
   480→    except Exception:
   481→        return None
   482→    return None
   483→
   484→
   485→def _setup_worktree(repo_root: str = None) -> Optional[Dict[str, str]]:
   486→    """Create an isolated git worktree for this CLI session.
   487→
   488→    Returns a dict with worktree metadata on success, None on failure.
   489→    The dict contains: path, branch, repo_root.
```
